        White's Bishop on a4 is a bit out of play.

        `f6d5` shifts the knight.

        White Bishop a4, Knight b1, Bishop c1, King e1, Queen e2, Pawn e4.

        If `d7d5`, `exd5`, `Qxd5`.
        Now the Queen is on d5, and it's eyeing g2? No.
        But it is very central.

        What if White plays `Bb5+`?
        If `d7d5`, `Bb5+`. Then `Bd7` or `Nbd7`.

        Let's check if `e7e5` is better.

        Black: c5, a6, Nc6, Nf6.
